For production code,\n *        always use parameter binding offered by the underlying\n *        database driver.\n */\ninline std::string sql_escape(std::string_view raw)\n{\n    std::string out;\n  return \"TRUE\";\n\n        std::string_view op_str = (op == BoolOperator::And) ? \"AND\" : \"OR\";\n\n        std::vector<std::string> child_sql;\n        child_sql.reserve(children.size());\n In production use, replace with proper\n *  full-text search (PostgreSQL tsvector/tsquery or Elasticsearch), but this\n *  algorithm is serviceable for demo purposes without extra dependencies.\n */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n    std::string word;\n    while (iss >> word)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(word);\n term_hits += it->second;\n    }\n\n    double score = static_cast<double>(term_hits) / static_cast<double>(word_freq.size() + 1);\n    return score;\n}\n\n/**\n *  Retrieve a highlighted snippet around the first term hit.\n */\nstd::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n                         std::size_t context = 40)\n{\n    std::string lowercase{ text };\n    util::ascii_to_lower(lowercase);\n\n    std::size_t best_pos = std::string_view::npos;\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n            best_pos = pos;\n    }\n\n    if (best_pos == std::string_view::npos)\n        return std::string(text.substr(0, std::min(text.size(), context))) + \"...\";\n\n    std::size_t start = (best_pos > context) ? best_pos - context : 0;\n (query.empty() || limit == 0) { return {}; }\n\n        // Tokenize once\n        const auto tokens = tokenize(query);\n\n        std::unordered_map<DocumentId, std::size_t> scoreTable;\n        {\n            std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> rlock(_indexMutex);\n\n            for (const auto& token : tokens) {\n                const auto it = _invertedIndex.find(token);\n                if (it == _invertedIndex.end()) { continue; }\n                for (const auto& docId : it->second) {\n                    ++scoreTable[docId];  // simple TF score\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Transform map to vector and sort by score descending\n        std::vector<std::pair<DocumentId, std::size_t>> ranked(scoreTable.begin(), scoreTable.end());\n std::string token;\n        token.reserve(32);\n\n        auto flush = [&]() {\n            if (!token.empty()) {\n                std::transform(token.begin(), token.end(), token.begin(),\n                               [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n                tokens.emplace_back(std::move(token));\n                token.clear();\n            }\n        };\n\n        for (char ch : text) {\n            if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch))) {\n                token.push_back(ch);\n            } else {\n                flush();\n            }\n        }\n        flush();\n        return tokens;\n    }\n\n    void indexDocument(const Job& job) {\n        const auto tokens = tokenize(job.text);\n\n        // Rebuild per-document token set to avoid duplicates\n        std::unordered_set<std::string> uniqueTokens(tokens.begin(), tokens.end());\n\n        {\n            std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> wlock(_indexMutex);\n\n (!_terminate.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {\n            Job currentJob;\n\n            {\n                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(_jobMutex);\n\n                _jobCv.wait(lock, [this] {\n                    return !_jobQueue.empty() || _terminate.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                });\n\n                if (_terminate.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) { break; }\n\n                currentJob = std::move(_jobQueue.front());\n                _jobQueue.pop();\n            }\n\n            // Process outside of lock to minimize contention\n            auto guard = ScopedGuard([&] {\n                // catch and log any thrown exception to prevent thread exit\n                if (std::uncaught_exceptions() > 0) {\n                    spdlog::error(\"[SearchIndexService] uncaught exception during indexing.\");\n                }\n            });\n\n            try {\n                indexDocument(currentJob);\n            } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n                spdlog::error(\"[SearchIndexService] Failed to index doc id={}, reason: {}\",\n                              currentJob.id, ex.what());\n            } catch (...) {\n                spdlog::error(\"[SearchIndexService] Unknown error while indexing doc id={}\",\n                              currentJob.id);\n            }\n        }\n        spdlog::info(\"[SearchIndexService] Worker thread exiting.\");\n    }\n\n    // State --------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Inverted index: word -> set<docId>\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unordered_set<DocumentId>> _invertedIndex;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex _indexMutex;\n\n    // Job queue\n    std::queue<Job>  _jobQueue;\n    std::mutex       _jobMutex;\n    std::condition_variable _jobCv;\n\n    // Worker\n    std::atomic<bool> _terminate;\n    std::thread       _worker;\n};\n\n// * @file SearchService.cpp\n * @brief Implementation of the centralized SearchService for IntraLedger BlogSuite\n *\n * This implementation consolidates search-related logic that was previously\n * scattered across multiple modules:\n *   - Tokenization logic from module_18.cpp and module_61.cpp\n *   - Inverted index management from module_41.cpp\n *   - Query processing and ranking\n *\n * Copyright © 2024 IntraLedger\n */\n\n#include \"SearchService.h\"\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <unordered_map>\n\nnamespace intraledger::search {\n\nSearchService& SearchService::instance() {\n    static SearchService instance;\n    return instance;\n}\n\nvoid SearchService::indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent) {\n    // Tokenize the document content\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(documentContent);\n\n    // Get unique tokens for this document\n    std::unordered_set<std::string> uniqueTokens(tokens.begin(), tokens.end());\n\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n    // If document was previously indexed, remove old entries\n    if (indexedDocuments.count(documentId) > 0) {\n        for (auto& [token, docIds] : invertedIndex) {\n            auto it = std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId);\n            if (it != docIds.end()) {\n                docIds.erase(it);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add new index entries\n    for (const auto& token : uniqueTokens) {\n        auto& docIds = invertedIndex[token];\n        if (std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId) == docIds.end()) {\n            docIds.push_back(documentId);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Track this document as indexed\n    indexedDocuments.insert(documentId);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> SearchService::query(const std::string& searchTerm) {\n    if (searchTerm.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    // Tokenize the search query\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(searchTerm);\n\n    if (tokens.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    // Score documents by number of matching terms (TF scoring)\n    std::unordered_map<int, std::size_t> scoreTable;\n\n    {\n        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n        for (const auto& token : tokens) {\n            auto it = invertedIndex.find(token);\n            if (it != invertedIndex.end()) {\n                for (int docId : it->second) {\n                    ++scoreTable[docId];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Convert to vector and sort by score (descending)\n    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::size_t>> ranked(scoreTable.begin(), scoreTable.end());\n    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n              [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second > b.second; });\n\n    // Extract document IDs in ranked order\n    std::vector<int> result;\n    result.reserve(ranked.size());\n    for (const auto& [docId, score] : ranked) {\n        result.push_back(docId);\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nvoid SearchService::removeDocument(int documentId) {\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n    // Remove document from all posting lists\n    for (auto& [token, docIds] : invertedIndex) {\n        auto it = std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId);\n        if (it != docIds.end()) {\n            docIds.erase(it);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Remove from tracked documents\n    indexedDocuments.erase(documentId);\n}\n\nvoid SearchService::clearIndex() {\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n    invertedIndex.clear();\n    indexedDocuments.clear();\n}\n\nstd::size_t SearchService::documentCount() const {\n    std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n    return indexedDocuments.size();\n}\n\nstd::vector<std::string> SearchService::tokenize(const std::string& text) {\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens;\n    std::string currentToken;\n    currentToken.reserve(32);\n\n    auto flushToken = [&]() {\n        if (currentToken.size() >= 2) {  // Filter out very short tokens\n            // Convert to lowercase\n            std::transform(currentToken.begin(), currentToken.end(), currentToken.begin(),\n                          [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n            tokens.push_back(std::move(currentToken));\n        }\n        currentToken.clear();\n    };\n\n    for (char ch : text) {\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch))) {\n            currentToken.push_back(ch);\n        } else {\n            flushToken();\n        }\n    }\n    flushToken();  // Don't forget the last token\n\n    return tokens;\n}\n\n} // namespace         if (_pos >= _query.size())\n                throw std::runtime_error(\"Unterminated quote in search query\");\n\n            std::string phrase;\n            phrase.reserve(_pos - start);\n (detail::iequals(word, \"OR\"))\n            return {TokenType::Or, word};\n        if (detail::iequals(word, \"NOT\"))\n            return {TokenType::Not, word};\n\n        return {TokenType::Word,     std::string_view close_tag = \"</mark>\")\n{\n    if (terms.empty())\n        return std::string(text);\n\n    // Build lowercase copy of the text for case-insensitive search\n    std::string The parser recognises:\n *\n *                 • Logical AND, OR, NOT   (case-insensitive, symbolic &&, ||, !)\n *                 • Parenthesis for precedence grouping\n *                 • Double-quoted phrases\n *                 • Plain words with automatic wildcard suffix\n *\n *               The output is safe for prepared statements; parameters are\n *               emitted -------------------------------------------------\n// Utilities\n// -------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace detail\n{\ninline std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n{\n    std::string out;\n    out.reserve(sv.size());\n    std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                   [](unsigned char ch) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch)); });\n    return out;\n}\n\ninline bool iequals(std::string_view a, std::string_view b)\n{\n    return to_lower(a) == to_lower(b);\n}\n\n} // namespace detail\n\n// -------------------------------------------------\n// Tokenizer\n// -------------------------------------------------\n\nenum class TokenType\n{\n    Word,\n    Phrase,\n    And,\n    Or,\n    Not,\n    LParen,\n    RParen,\n    End,\n};\n\nstruct Token\n{\n    TokenType            type;\n    std::string          lexeme;\n};\n\nclass Tokenizer\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Tokenizer(std::string_view q) ++_pos;\n            return {TokenType::Not, \"!\"};\n        }\n\n        // Quoted phrase\n        if (c == '\"')\n        {\n            ++_pos;\n            std::size_t start = _pos;\n           else\n                    ++_pos;\n            }\n\n            if (_pos >= _query.size())\n                throw std::runtime_error(\"Unterminated quote in search query\");\n\n            std::string phrase;\n            phrase.reserve(_pos - start);\n            for (std::size_t i = start; i < _pos; ++i)\n            {\n                char ch = _query[i];\n                if (ch == '\\\\' && i + 1 < _pos)\n                {\n                    phrase.push_back(_query[i + 1]);\n                    ++i;\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    phrase.push_back(ch);\n                }\n            }\n\n            ++_pos; // Skip closing quote\n            return {TokenType::Phrase, phrase};\n        }\n\n        // Word or textual \"NOT\"))\n            return {TokenType::Not, word};\n\n        return {TokenType::Word, std::move(word)};\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    static bool is_word_char(char c)\n    {\n        return std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) || c == '_' || c == '-';\n    }\n\n    char peek(std::size_t offset) const\n    {\n        if (_pos + offset >= _query.size())\n            return '\\0';\n        return _query[_pos + offset];\n    }\n\n    void consume_ws()\n    {\n        while (_pos < _query.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(_query[_pos])))\n            ++_pos;\n        visit(node->left.get());\n        _buffer += \" AND \";\n        visit(node->right.get());\n        _buffer += \")\";\n    }\n\n    void visit_or(const OrNode* node)\n    {\n        _buffer += For production code,\n *        always use parameter binding offered by the underlying\n *        database driver.\n */\ninline std::string sql_escape(std::string_view raw)\n{\n    std::string out;\n */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n    std::string word;\n    while (iss >> word)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(word);\n around the first term hit.\n */\nstd::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n                         std::size_t context = std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n            best_pos = pos;\n    }\n\n    if }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Recursive-descent parser for simple boolean query grammar\n// Supports:\n//      term           -> WORD | \"PHRASE\"\n//      factor         -> [NOT] term\n//      expr_and       -> factor { AND factor }\n//      expr_or        -> expr_and { OR expr_and }\n//      query          -> nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n        while (match_keyword(\"or\"))\n            nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n\n        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n            return nodes.front();\n        return m_pos);\n            m_pos = end + 1;\n            return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(phrase), negated);\n        }\n\n        // Word\n        std::size_t start = m_pos;\n        while */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n reinterpret_cast<const utf8proc_uint8_t*>(src.data());\n   120→    utf8proc_uint8_t* result = nullptr;\n   121→\n   122→    auto len = utf8proc_decompose(data, src.size(), &result,\n   123→                                  UTF8PROC_NULLTERM | UTF8PROC_COMPOSE);\n   124→\n   125→    if (len < 0)\n   126→        throw std::runtime_error(\"utf8proc_decompose() failed\");\n   127→\n   128→    std::string normalized(reinterpret_cast<char*>(result), len);\n   129→    free(result);\n   130→    return normalized;\n   131→}\n   132→#endif\n   133→\n   134→} // namespace util\n   135→\n   136→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   137→// Search query AST (kept for SQL-based search compatibility)\n   138→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   139→\n   140→enum class BoolOperator\n   141→{\n   142→    And,\n   143→    Or\n   144→};\n   145→\n   146→// Forward declaration\n   147→struct Node;\n   148→\n   149→using NodePtr = std::shared_ptr<Node>;\n   150→\n   151→/**\n   152→ *  Base search AST node\n   153→ */\n   154→struct Node\n   155→{\n   156→    virtual ~Node() = default;\n   157→    virtual std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const = 0;\n   158→};\n   159→\n   160→/**\n   161→ *  Represents a single token (word, phrase) possibly preceded by NOT.\n   162→ */\n   163→struct TermNode final : public Node\n   164→{\n   165→    std::string  token;\n   166→    bool         negated { false };\n   167→\n   168→    explicit TermNode(std::string token_, bool neg = false)\n   169→        : token(std::move(token_)), negated(neg) {}\n   170→\n   171→    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n   172→    {\n   173→        std::string like_pattern = fmt::format(\"%{}%\", util::sql_escape(token));\n   174→        out_params.push_back(like_pattern);\n   175→\n   176→        if (negated)\n   177→            return \"content NOT ILIKE ?\";\n   178→        else\n   179→            return \"content ILIKE ?\";\n   180→    }\n   181→};\n   182→\n   183→/**\n   184→ *  Logical grouping node (AND / OR)\n   185→ */\n   186→struct BoolNode final : public Node\n   187→{\n   188→    BoolOperator                op;\n   189→    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Node>> children;\n   190→\n   191→    BoolNode(BoolOperator op_, std::vector<NodePtr> ch)\n   192→        : op(op_), children(std::move(ch)) {}\n   193→\n   194→    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n   195→    {\n   196→        if (children.empty())\n   197→            return \"TRUE\";\n   198→\n   199→        std::string_view op_str = (op == BoolOperator::And) ? \"AND\" : \"OR\";\n   200→\n   201→        std::vector<std::string> child_sql;\n   202→        child_sql.reserve(children.size());\n   203→        for (auto const& c : children)\n   204→            child_sql.push_back(c->to_sql(out_params));\n   205→\n   206→        return fmt::format(\"({})\",\n   207→                           fmt::join(child_sql, fmt::format(\" {} \", op_str)));\n   208→    }\n   209→};\n   210→\n   211→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   212→// Recursive-descent parser for simple boolean query grammar\n   213→// Supports:\n   214→//      term           -> WORD | \"PHRASE\"\n   215→//      factor         -> [NOT] term\n   216→//      expr_and       -> factor { AND factor }\n   217→//      expr_or        -> expr_and { OR expr_and }\n   218→//      query          -> expr_or\n   219→//--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   220→\n   221→class QueryParser\n   222→{\n   223→public:\n   224→    explicit QueryParser(std::string_view q)\n   225→        : m_query(q)\n   226→    {\n   227→        util::ascii_to_lower(m_query); // case-folding\n   228→    }\n   229→\n   230→    NodePtr parse()\n   231→    {\n   232→        m_pos = 0;\n   233→        NodePtr root = parse_or();\n   234→        consume_whitespace();\n   235→        if (m_pos != m_query.size())\n   236→            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected trailing characters\");\n   237→        return root;\n   238→    }\n   239→\n   240→private:\n   241→    std::string m_query;\n   242→    std::size_t m_pos { 0 };\n   243→\n   244→    [[nodiscard]] bool eof() const noexcept { return m_pos >= m_query.size(); }\n   245→\n   246→    void consume_whitespace()\n   247→    {\n   248→        while (!eof() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n   249→            ++m_pos;\n   250→    }\n   251→\n   252→    bool match_keyword(std::string_view kw)\n   253→    {\n   254→        consume_whitespace();\n   255→        if (m_query.compare(m_pos, kw.size(), kw) == 0)\n   256→        {\n   257→            const auto next = m_pos + kw.size();\n   258→            if (next == m_query.size() || std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[next])))\n   259→            {\n   260→                m_pos = next;\n   261→                return true;\n   262→            }\n   263→        }\n   264→        return false;\n   265→    }\n   266→\n   267→    NodePtr parse_or()\n   268→    {\n   269→        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n   270→        nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n   271→        while (match_keyword(\"or\"))\n   272→            nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n   273→\n   274→        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n   275→            return nodes.front();\n   276→        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::Or, std::move(nodes));\n   277→    }\n   278→\n   279→    NodePtr parse_and()\n   280→    {\n   281→        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n   282→        nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n   283→        while (match_keyword(\"and\"))\n   284→            nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n   285→\n   286→        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n   287→            return nodes.front();\n   288→        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::And, std::move(nodes));\n   289→    }\n   290→\n   291→    NodePtr parse_factor()\n   292→    {\n   293→        consume_whitespace();\n   294→        bool neg = false;\n   295→        if (match_keyword(\"not\"))\n   296→            neg = true;\n   297→\n   298→ std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected end of query\");\n   306→\n   307→        char ch = m_query[m_pos];\n   308→        if (ch == '\"')\n   309→        {\n   310→            ++m_pos;\n   311→            std::size_t end = m_query.find('\"', m_pos);\n   312→            if (end == std::string::npos)\n   313→                throw std::runtime_error(\"Unterminated quote\");\n   314→\n   315→ 319→\n   320→        // Word\n   321→        std::size_t start = m_pos;\n   322→        while (!eof() && !std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n   323→            ++m_pos;\n   324→\n   325→        std::string word = m_query.substr(start, m_pos - start);\n   326→        return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(word), negated);\n   327→    }\n   328→};\n   329→\n   330→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   331→// Result DTO & Repository interface  (part of broader system)\n   332→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   333→\n   334→struct ArticleDTO\n   335→{\n   336→    std::int64_t             id              {};\n   337→ 353→\n   354→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   355→// Ranking & Snippet Extraction\n   356→//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   357→\n   358→namespace ranking {\n   359→\n   360→/**\n   361→ *  Very naive TF-IDF style scoring.  In production use, replace with proper\n   362→ *  full-text search (PostgreSQL tsvector/tsquery or Elasticsearch), but this\n   363→ *  algorithm is serviceable for demo purposes without extra dependencies.\n   364→ */\n   365→double score_document(std::string_view text,\n   366→                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n   367→{\n   368→    if (search_terms.empty())\n   369→        return 0.0;\n   370→\n   371→    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n   372→    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n   373→    std::string  387→\n   388→    double score = static_cast<double>(term_hits) / static_cast<double>(word_freq.size() + 1);\n   389→    return score;\n   390→}\n   391→\n   392→/**\n   393→ *  Retrieve a highlighted snippet around the first term hit.\n   394→ */\n   395→std::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n   396→                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n   397→                         std::size_t context = 40)\n   398→{\n   399→    std::string lowercase{ text };\n   400→    util::ascii_to_lower(lowercase);\n   401→\n   402→    std::size_t best_pos = std::string_view::npos;\n   403→    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n   404→    {\n   405→        std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n   406→        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n   407→            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n   408→            best_pos = pos;\n   409→    }\n   410→\n   411→    if (best_pos == std::string_view::npos)\n   412→        return std::string(text.substr(0, std::min(text.size(), context))) + \"...\";\n   413→\n   414→    std::size_t start = (best_pos > context) ? best_pos - context : 0;\n   415→    std::size_t end   = std::min(text.size(), best_pos + context);\n   416→\n   417→    std::string snippet = std::string(text.substr(start, end - start));\n   418→    // crude HTML highlight\n   419→    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n   420→    {\n   421→        std::regex rgx(term, std::regex_constants::icase);\n   422→        snippet = std::regex_replace(snippet, rgx, \"<mark>$&</mark>\");\n   423→    }\n   424→\n   425→    if (start != 0)\n   426→        snippet.insert(0, \"...\");\n   427→    if (end != text.size())\n   428→        snippet.append(\"...\");\n   429→    return snippet;\n   430→}\n For production code,\n *        always use parameter binding offered by the underlying\n *        database driver.\n */\ninline std::string sql_escape(std::string_view raw)\n{\n    std::string out;\n    out.reserve(raw.size() + 2); // cheap guess\n    for (char ch : raw)\n    {\n        if (ch == '\\'' || ch == '\\\"' || ch == '\\\\')\n            out.push_back('\\\\');\n        out.push_back(ch);\n    }\n    return out;\n}\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n/**\n *  Normalize UTF-8 string into NFC.\n */\ninline std::string normalize_utf8(std::string_view src)\n{\n &result,\n                                  UTF8PROC_NULLTERM | UTF8PROC_COMPOSE);\n\n    if (len < 0)\n        throw std::runtime_error(\"utf8proc_decompose() failed\");\n\n    std::string normalized(reinterpret_cast<char*>(result), len);\n    free(result);\n    return normalized;\n}\n#endif\n\n} // namespace util\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Search query AST (kept for SQL-based search compatibility)\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nenum class BoolOperator\n{\n    And,\n    Or\n};\n\n// Forward declaration\nstruct Node;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::shared_ptr<Node>;\n\n/**\n *  Base search AST node\n */\nstruct Node\n{\n    virtual ~Node() = default;\n    virtual std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const = 0;\n};\n\n/**\n *  Represents a single token (word, phrase) possibly preceded by NOT.\n */\nstruct TermNode final : public Node\n{\n    std::string  token;\n    bool         negated { false };\n\n    explicit TermNode(std::string token_, bool neg = false)\n        : token(std::move(token_)), negated(neg) {}\n\n    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n    {\n        std::string like_pattern = fmt::format(\"%{}%\", util::sql_escape(token));\n        out_params.push_back(like_pattern);\n\n        if (negated)\n            return \"content NOT ILIKE ?\";\n        else\n            return \"content ILIKE ?\";\n    }\n};\n\n/**\n *  Logical grouping node (AND / OR)\n */\nstruct BoolNode final : public Node\n{\n    BoolOperator                op;\n    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Node>> children;\n\n    BoolNode(BoolOperator op_, std::vector<NodePtr> ch)\n        : op(op_), children(std::move(ch)) {}\n\n    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n    {\n        if (children.empty())\n            return \"TRUE\";\n\n        std::string_view op_str = (op == BoolOperator::And) ? \"AND\" : \"OR\";\n\n        std::vector<std::string> child_sql;\n        child_sql.reserve(children.size());\n        for (auto const& c : children)\n            child_sql.push_back(c->to_sql(out_params));\n\n        return fmt::format(\"({})\",\n                           fmt::join(child_sql, fmt::format(\" {} \", op_str)));\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Recursive-descent parser for simple boolean query grammar\n// Supports:\n//      term           -> WORD | \"PHRASE\"\n//      factor         -> [NOT] term\n//      expr_and       -> factor { AND factor }\n//      expr_or        -> expr_and { OR expr_and }\n//      query          -> expr_or\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass QueryParser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit QueryParser(std::string_view q)\n        : m_query(q)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(m_query); // case-folding\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse()\n    {\n        m_pos = 0;\n        NodePtr root = parse_or();\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (m_pos != m_query.size())\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected trailing characters\");\n        return root;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::string m_query;\n    std::size_t m_pos { 0 };\n\n    [[nodiscard]] bool eof() const noexcept { return m_pos >= m_query.size(); }\n\n    void consume_whitespace()\n    {\n        while (!eof() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n            ++m_pos;\n    }\n\n    bool match_keyword(std::string_view kw)\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (m_query.compare(m_pos, kw.size(), kw) == 0)\n        {\n            const auto next = m_pos + kw.size();\n            if (next == m_query.size() || std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[next])))\n            {\n                m_pos = next;\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n        nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n        while (match_keyword(\"or\"))\n            nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n\n        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n            return nodes.front();\n        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::Or, std::move(nodes));\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n        nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n        while (match_keyword(\"and\"))\n            nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n\n        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n            return nodes.front();\n        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::And, std::move(nodes));\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_factor()\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        bool neg = false;\n        if (match_keyword(\"not\"))\n            neg = true;\n\n        return parse_term(neg);\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_term(bool negated)\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (eof())\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected end of query\");\n\n        char ch = m_query[m_pos];\n        if (ch == '\"')\n        {\n            ++m_pos;\n            std::size_t end = m_query.find('\"', m_pos);\n            if (end == std::string::npos)\n                throw std::runtime_error(\"Unterminated quote\");\n\n            std::string phrase = m_query.substr(m_pos, end - m_pos);\n            m_pos = end + 1;\n            return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(phrase), negated);\n        }\n\n        // Word\n        std::size_t start = m_pos;\n        while (!eof() && !std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n            ++m_pos;\n\n        std::string word = m_query.substr(start, m_pos - start);\n        return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(word), negated);\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Result DTO & Repository interface  (part of broader system)\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct ArticleDTO\n{\n    std::int64_t             id              {};\n    std::string              title;\n    std::string              content;        // raw HTML or Markdown\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point published_at;\n};\n\nclass IArticleRepository\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IArticleRepository() = default;\n\n    virtual std::vector<ArticleDTO> search(\n        std::string_view   sql_where_clause,\n        const std::vector<std::string>& params,\n        std::size_t        limit,\n        std::size_t        offset) = 0;\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Ranking & Snippet Extraction\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace ranking {\n\n/**\n *  Very naive TF-IDF style scoring.  In production use, replace with proper\n *  full-text search (PostgreSQL tsvector/tsquery or Elasticsearch), but this\n *  algorithm is serviceable for demo purposes without extra dependencies.\n */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n    std::string word;\n    while (iss >> word)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(word);\n        ++word_freq[word];\n    }\n\n    int term_hits = 0;\n    for (auto const& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        auto it = word_freq.find(term);\n        if (it != word_freq.end())\n            term_hits += it->second;\n    }\n\n    double score = static_cast<double>(term_hits) / static_cast<double>(word_freq.size() + 1);\n    return score;\n}\n\n/**\n *  Retrieve a highlighted snippet around the first term hit.\n */\nstd::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n                         std::size_t context = 40)\n{\n    std::string lowercase{ text };\n    util::ascii_to_lower(lowercase);\n\n    std::size_t best_pos = std::string_view::npos;\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n            best_pos = pos;\n    }\n\n    if (best_pos == std::string_view::npos)\n        return std::string(text.substr(0, std::min(text.size(), context))) + \"...\";\n\n    std::size_t start = (best_pos > context) ? best_pos - context : 0;\n    std::size_t end   = std::min(text.size(), best_pos + context);\n\n    std::string snippet = std::string(text.substr(start, end - start));\n    // crude HTML highlight\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::regex rgx(term, std::regex_constants::icase);\n        snippet = std::regex_replace(snippet, rgx, \"<mark>$&</mark>\");\n    }\n\n    if (start != 0)\n        snippet.insert(0, \"...\");\n    if (end != text.size())\n        snippet.append(\"...\");\n    return snippet;\n}\n\n} // namespace ranking\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// SQL-based Search Service (for database queries)\n// Uses the centralized SearchService for in-memory index operations\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass DatabaseSearchService\n{\npublic:\n    explicit DatabaseSearchService(std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> repo)\n        : m_repo(std::move(repo))\n    {\n        if (!m_repo)\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"DatabaseSearchService requires a repository\");\n    }\n\n    struct Result\n    {\n        ArticleDTO  article;\n        double      score;\n        std::string snippet;\n    };\n\n    /**\n     * Search using database SQL queries (for complex boolean queries)\n     */\n    std::vector<Result> search(std::string_view query,\n                               std::size_t limit  = 50,\n                               std::size_t offset = 0)\n    {\n        if (query.size() < 2)\n            return {}; // ignore noise\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n        query = util::normalize_utf8(query);\n#endif\n\n        // Step 1: Parse\n        std::vector<std::string> bound_params;\n        auto ast  = QueryParser{query}.parse();\n        auto where_clause = ast->to_sql(bound_params);\n\n        // Step 2: Fetch data\n        auto records = m_repo->search(where_clause, bound_params, limit, offset);\n\n        // Step 3: Score + snippet\n        std::vector<std::string> search_terms = collect_terms(ast);\n        std::vector<Result> out;\n        out.reserve(records.size());\n\n        for (auto&& rec : records)\n        {\n            double sc = ranking::score_document(rec.content, search_terms);\n            std::string snip = ranking::make_snippet(rec.content, search_terms);\n            out.push_back(Result{std::move(rec), sc, std::move(snip)});\n        }\n\n        // Step 4: Sort by score desc, then published_at desc\n        std::ranges::stable_sort(out, [](auto const& a, auto const& b)\n        {\n            if (a.score != b.score)\n                return a.score > b.score;\n            return a.article.published_at > b.article.published_at;\n        });\n\n        spdlog::info(\"Database search for '{}' returned {} documents\", query, out.size());\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Search using the centralized in-memory index (for fast lookups)\n     * Delegates to the centralized SearchService\n     */\n    std::vector<int> searchIndex(const std::string& query)\n    {\n        return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(query);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Index a document using the centralized SearchService\n     * Delegates to the centralized SearchService\n     */\n    void indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& content)\n    {\n        intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, content);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> m_repo;\n\n    static void collect_terms_recursive(const NodePtr& node,\n                                        std::vector<std::string>& dst)\n    {\n        if (!node)\n            return;\n\n        if (auto term = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<TermNode>(node))\n        {\n            dst.push_back(term->token);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (auto group = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<BoolNode>(node))\n        {\n            for (auto const& ch : group->children)\n                collect_terms_recursive(ch, dst);\n        }\n    }\n\n    static std::vector<std::string> collect_terms(const NodePtr& ast)\n    {\n        std::vector<std::string> terms;\n        collect_terms_recursive(ast, terms);\n        return terms;\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Convenience functions using the centralized SearchService\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * @brief Index a document using the centralized SearchService\n */\nvoid indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Query documents using the centralized SearchService\n */\nstd::vector<int> queryDocuments(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Dummy repository for demonstration / unit ---------------------\n *  File:        src/module_41.cpp\n *  Author:      IntraLedger Engineering\n *\n *  Description:\n *      Core search subsystem responsible for parsing free-form user queries,\n *      translating them into safe SQL fragments understood by the ORM, executing\n *      the query, and finally scoring & highlighting the resulting documents.\n *\n *      The goal of this module is to keep search-related logic well‐encapsulated\n *      and agnostic to the underlying persistence implementation (MariaDB /\n *      PostgreSQL).  All DB interaction is performed through the generic\n *      `IArticleRepository` interface defined in the Repository layer.\n *\n *      Dependencies:\n *          – C++20 STL\n *          – fmt            (header-only; used for safe SQL construction)\n *          – utf8proc       (optional; compile-time flag SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE)\n *          – spdlog         (header-only; logging)\n *\n *      Thread-safety:\n *          All public functions are thread-safe and may be accessed from the\n *          async job processor or regular request threads concurrently.  The\n *          service makes no global mutations and relies on immutable data\n *          structures as much as possible.\n *\n *      Build:\n *          # CMake\n *          target_link_libraries(web_blog\n *              PRIVATE fmt::fmt spdlog::spdlog)\n *\n *  License: MIT\n ********************************************************************/\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <charconv>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <memory>\n#include <optional>\n#include <ranges>\n#include <regex>\n#include <sstream>\n#include <stdexcept>\n#include <string>\n#include <string_view>\n#include <unordered_map>\n#include <variant>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <fmt/core.h>\n#include <fmt/ranges.h>\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n    #include <utf8proc.h>\n#endif\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace intraledger::blogsuite::search {\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helper utilities\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace util {\n\n/**\n *  Convert string to lower-case in place (ASCII only).\n */\ninline void ascii_to_lower(std::string& s)\n{\n    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(),\n                   [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n}\n\n/**\n *  Perform lightweight quoting to protect against SQL injection\n *  when constructing raw SQL fragments.  The ORM still binds the\n *  parameters using prepared statements, but defensive programming\n *  never hurts.\n *\n *  @note This is NOT a full SQL sanitizer.  For production code,\n *        always use parameter binding offered by the underlying\n *        database driver.\n */\ninline std::string sql_escape(std::string_view raw)\n{\n    std::string out;\n    out.reserve(raw.size() + 2); // cheap guess\n    for (char ch : raw)\n    {\n        if (ch == '\\'' || ch == '\\\"' || ch == '\\\\')\n            out.push_back('\\\\');\n        out.push_back(ch);\n    }\n    return out;\n}\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n/**\n *  Normalize UTF-8 string into NFC.\n */\ninline std::string normalize_utf8(std::string_view src)\n{\n    utf8proc_uint8_t const* data =\n        reinterpret_cast<const utf8proc_uint8_t*>(src.data());\n    utf8proc_uint8_t* result = nullptr;\n\n    auto len = utf8proc_decompose(data, src.size(), &result,\n                                  UTF8PROC_NULLTERM | UTF8PROC_COMPOSE);\n\n    if (len < 0)\n        throw std::runtime_error(\"utf8proc_decompose() failed\");\n\n    std::string normalized(reinterpret_cast<char*>(result), len);\n    free(result);\n    return normalized;\n}\n#endif\n\n} // namespace util\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Search query AST\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nenum class BoolOperator\n{\n    And,\n    Or\n};\n\n// Forward declaration\nstruct Node;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::shared_ptr<Node>;\n\n/**\n *  Base search AST node\n */\nstruct Node\n{\n    virtual ~Node() = default;\n    virtual std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const = 0;\n};\n\n/**\n *  Represents a single token (word, phrase) possibly preceded by NOT.\n */\nstruct TermNode final : public Node\n{\n    std::string  token;\n    bool         negated { false };\n\n    explicit TermNode(std::string token_, bool neg = false)\n        : token(std::move(token_)), negated(neg) {}\n\n    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n    {\n        std::string like_pattern = fmt::format(\"%{}%\", util::sql_escape(token));\n        out_params.push_back(like_pattern);\n\n        if (negated)\n            return \"content NOT ILIKE ?\";\n        else\n            return \"content ILIKE ?\";\n    }\n};\n\n/**\n *  Logical grouping node (AND / OR)\n */\nstruct BoolNode final : public Node\n{\n    BoolOperator                op;\n    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Node>> children;\n\n    BoolNode(BoolOperator op_, std::vector<NodePtr> ch)\n        : op(op_), children(std::move(ch)) {}\n\n    std::string to_sql(std::vector<std::string>& out_params) const override\n    {\n        if (children.empty())\n            return \"TRUE\";\n\n        std::string_view op_str = (op == BoolOperator::And) ? \"AND\" : \"OR\";\n\n        std::vector<std::string> child_sql;\n        child_sql.reserve(children.size());\n        for (auto const& c : children)\n            child_sql.push_back(c->to_sql(out_params));\n\n        return fmt::format(\"({})\",\n                           fmt::join(child_sql, fmt::format(\" {} \", op_str)));\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Recursive-descent parser for simple boolean query grammar\n// Supports:\n//      term           -> WORD | \"PHRASE\"\n//      factor         -> [NOT] term\n//      expr_and       -> factor { AND factor }\n//      expr_or        -> expr_and { OR expr_and }\n//      query          -> expr_or\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass QueryParser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit QueryParser(std::string_view q)\n        : m_query(q)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(m_query); // case-folding\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse()\n    {\n        m_pos = 0;\n        NodePtr root = parse_or();\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (m_pos != m_query.size())\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected trailing characters\");\n        return root;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::string m_query;\n    std::size_t m_pos { 0 };\n\n    [[nodiscard]] bool eof() const noexcept { return m_pos >= m_query.size(); }\n\n    void consume_whitespace()\n    {\n        while (!eof() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n            ++m_pos;\n    }\n\n    bool match_keyword(std::string_view kw)\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (m_query.compare(m_pos, kw.size(), kw) == 0)\n        {\n            const auto next = m_pos + kw.size();\n            if (next == m_query.size() || std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[next])))\n            {\n                m_pos = next;\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n        nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n        while (match_keyword(\"or\"))\n            nodes.push_back(parse_and());\n\n        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n            return nodes.front();\n        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::Or, std::move(nodes));\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        std::vector<NodePtr> nodes;\n        nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n        while (match_keyword(\"and\"))\n            nodes.push_back(parse_factor());\n\n        if (nodes.size() == 1)\n            return nodes.front();\n        return std::make_shared<BoolNode>(BoolOperator::And, std::move(nodes));\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_factor()\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        bool neg = false;\n        if (match_keyword(\"not\"))\n            neg = true;\n\n        return parse_term(neg);\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse_term(bool negated)\n    {\n        consume_whitespace();\n        if (eof())\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unexpected end of query\");\n\n        char ch = m_query[m_pos];\n        if (ch == '\"')\n        {\n            ++m_pos;\n            std::size_t end = m_query.find('\"', m_pos);\n            if (end == std::string::npos)\n                throw std::runtime_error(\"Unterminated quote\");\n\n            std::string phrase = m_query.substr(m_pos, end - m_pos);\n            m_pos = end + 1;\n            return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(phrase), negated);\n        }\n\n        // Word\n        std::size_t start = m_pos;\n        while (!eof() && !std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_query[m_pos])))\n            ++m_pos;\n\n        std::string word = m_query.substr(start, m_pos - start);\n        return std::make_shared<TermNode>(std::move(word), negated);\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Result DTO & Repository interface  (part of broader system)\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct ArticleDTO\n{\n    std::int64_t             id              {};\n    std::string              title;\n    std::string              content;        // raw HTML or Markdown\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point published_at;\n};\n\nclass IArticleRepository\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IArticleRepository() = default;\n\n    virtual std::vector<ArticleDTO> search(\n        std::string_view   sql_where_clause,\n        const std::vector<std::string>& params,\n        std::size_t        limit,\n        std::size_t        offset) = 0;\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Ranking & Snippet Extraction\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace ranking {\n\n/**\n *  Very naive TF-IDF style scoring.  In production use, replace with proper\n *  full-text search (PostgreSQL tsvector/tsquery or Elasticsearch), but this\n *  algorithm is serviceable for demo purposes without extra dependencies.\n */\ndouble score_document(std::string_view text,\n                      const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms)\n{\n    if (search_terms.empty())\n        return 0.0;\n\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> word_freq;\n    std::istringstream iss{std::string(text)};\n    std::string word;\n    while (iss >> word)\n    {\n        util::ascii_to_lower(word);\n        ++word_freq[word];\n    }\n\n    int term_hits = 0;\n    for (auto const& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        auto it = word_freq.find(term);\n        if (it != word_freq.end())\n            term_hits += it->second;\n    }\n\n    double score = static_cast<double>(term_hits) / static_cast<double>(word_freq.size() + 1);\n    return score;\n}\n\n/**\n *  Retrieve a highlighted snippet around the first term hit.\n */\nstd::string make_snippet(std::string_view text,\n                         const std::vector<std::string>& search_terms,\n                         std::size_t context = 40)\n{\n    std::string lowercase{ text };\n    util::ascii_to_lower(lowercase);\n\n    std::size_t best_pos = std::string_view::npos;\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::size_t pos = lowercase.find(term);\n        if (pos != std::string_view::npos &&\n            (best_pos == std::string_view::npos || pos < best_pos))\n            best_pos = pos;\n    }\n\n    if (best_pos == std::string_view::npos)\n        return std::string(text.substr(0, std::min(text.size(), context))) + \"...\";\n\n    std::size_t start = (best_pos > context) ? best_pos - context : 0;\n    std::size_t end   = std::min(text.size(), best_pos + context);\n\n    std::string snippet = std::string(text.substr(start, end - start));\n    // crude HTML highlight\n    for (const auto& term : search_terms)\n    {\n        std::regex rgx(term, std::regex_constants::icase);\n        snippet = std::regex_replace(snippet, rgx, \"<mark>$&</mark>\");\n    }\n\n    if (start != 0)\n        snippet.insert(0, \"...\");\n    if (end != text.size())\n        snippet.append(\"...\");\n    return snippet;\n}\n\n} // namespace ranking\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Search Service\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass SearchService\n{\npublic:\n    explicit SearchService(std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> repo)\n        : m_repo(std::move(repo))\n    {\n        if (!m_repo)\n            throw std::invalid_argument(\"SearchService requires a repository\");\n    }\n\n    struct Result\n    {\n        ArticleDTO  article;\n        double      score;\n        std::string snippet;\n    };\n\n    std::vector<Result> search(std::string_view query,\n                               std::size_t limit  = 50,\n                               std::size_t offset = 0)\n    {\n        if (query.size() < 2)\n            return {}; // ignore noise\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_UTF8_NORMALIZE\n        query = util::normalize_utf8(query);\n#endif\n\n        // Step 1: Parse\n        std::vector<std::string> bound_params;\n        auto ast  = QueryParser{query}.parse();\n        auto where_clause = ast->to_sql(bound_params);\n\n        // Step 2: Fetch data\n        auto records = m_repo->search(where_clause, bound_params, limit, offset);\n\n        // Step 3: Score + snippet\n        std::vector<std::string> search_terms = collect_terms(ast);\n        std::vector<Result> out;\n        out.reserve(records.size());\n\n        for (auto&& rec : records)\n        {\n            double sc = ranking::score_document(rec.content, search_terms);\n            std::string snip = ranking::make_snippet(rec.content, search_terms);\n            out.push_back(Result{std::move(rec), sc, std::move(snip)});\n        }\n\n        // Step 4: Sort by score desc, then published_at desc\n        std::ranges::stable_sort(out, [](auto const& a, auto const& b)\n        {\n            if (a.score != b.score)\n                return a.score > b.score;\n            return a.article.published_at > b.article.published_at;\n        });\n\n        spdlog::info(\"Search for '{}' returned {} documents\", query, out.size());\n        return out;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::shared_ptr<IArticleRepository> m_repo;\n\n    static void collect_terms_recursive(const NodePtr& node,\n                                        std::vector<std::string>& dst)\n    {\n        if (!node)\n            return;\n\n        if (auto term = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<TermNode>(node))\n        {\n            dst.push_back(term->token);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (auto group = std::dynamic_pointer_cast<BoolNode>(node))\n        {\n            for (auto const& ch : group->children)\n                collect_terms_recursive(ch, dst);\n        }\n    }\n\n    static std::vector<std::string> collect_terms(const NodePtr& ast)\n    {\n        std::vector<std::string> terms;\n        collect_terms_recursive(ast, terms);\n        return terms;\n    }\n};\n\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Dummy repository for demonstration / unit tests\n//------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#ifdef SEARCH_MODULE_SELFTEST\n\nclass InMemoryArticleRepo final : public IArticleRepository\n{\npublic:\n    std::vector<ArticleDTO> storage;\n\n    std::vector<ArticleDTO> search(std::string_view   /*where_clause*/,\n                                   const std::vector<std::string>& params,\n                                   std::size_t        limit,\n                                   std::size_t        offset) override\n    {\n        std::vector<ArticleDTO> out;\n        for (auto const& a : storage)\n        {\n            bool match = true;\n            for (auto const& p : params)\n            {\n                std::string needle = p;\n                needle.erase(std::remove(needle.begin(), needle.end(), '%'),\n                             needle.end());\n                std::string content_lc = a.content;\n                util::ascii_to_lower(content_lc);\n                match &= content_lc.find(needle) != std::string::npos;\n            }\n            if (match)\n                out.push_back(a);\n        }\n\n        if (offset >= out.size())\n            return {};\n        auto begin = out.begin() + offset;\n        auto end   = (limit == 0) ? out.end() : begin + std::min(limit, out.size() - offset);\n        return std::vector<ArticleDTO>(begin, end);\n    }\n};\n\n#include <iostream>\n\nstatic void run_selftest()\n{\n    auto repo = std::make_shared<InMemoryArticleRepo>();\n    repo->storage = {\n        {1, \"Hello World\",  \"This is a test article about C++ and world news.\",\n         std::chrono::system_clock::now()},\n        {2, \"Cats & Dogs\",  \"All about pets and how to train dogs.\",\n         std::chrono::system_clock::now()},\n        {3, \"Advanced C++\", \"Templates, metaprogramming, and beyond.\",\n         std::chrono::system_clock::now()}\n    };\n\n    SearchService score=\" << r.score << \"\\nSnippet:\\n\"\n                  << r.snippet << \"\\n\\n\";\n}\n\nint main()\n{\n    run_selftest();\n}\n\n#endif // SEARCH_MODULE_SELFTEST\n\n} // namespace intraledger::blogsuite::search\n```"}}

return { TokenType::Colon, \":\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '(':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::LParen, \"(\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case ')':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::RParen, \")\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '\"':\n                return lex_phrase();\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        // Word or reserved keyword\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch)) || ch == '_' || ch == '-')\n        {\n            auto word = read_while([](unsigned char c) {\n                return std::isalnum(c) || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '.';\n            });\n\n            const auto lower = to_lower(word);\n            if (lower == \"and\")\n                return { TokenType::And, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"or\")\n                return { TokenType::Or, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"not\")\n                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n\n            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n    }\n\n    Token lex_phrase()\n    {\n        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        bool escaped = false;\n\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n        {\n            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n            if (escaped)\n            {\n                // Accept common escape sequences or keep char as is\n                switch (ch)\n                {\n                    case '\"':  oss << '\"'; break;\n                    case '\\\\': oss << '\\\\'; break;\n                    case 'n':  oss << '\\n'; break;\n                    case 't':  oss << '\\t'; break;\n                    default:   oss << ch;  break;\n                }\n                escaped = false;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\\\\')\n            {\n                escaped = true;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\"')\n            {\n                // End of phrase\n                return { TokenType::Phrase, oss.str(), start_pos };\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                oss << ch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Reached end-of-input without closing quote\n        throw ParseError { \"Unterminated string literal\", start_pos };\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  AST definitions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct QueryNode;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n\nstruct Term\n{\n    std::string         || std::holds_alternative<Not>(*this);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Parser (recursive-descent)\n//
