*  IntraLedger BlogSuite : Search Query Parsing & SQL Translation Module\n *  ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n *\n *  File:        src/module_61.cpp\n *  Author:      IntraLedger Core Team\n *  Description: Implements a light-weight domain-specific language (DSL) that\n *               allows end-users to perform advanced full-text search queries\n *               similar to what you would expect from modern SaaS products.\n *\n *               Example query language features:\n *                 • Logical operators:  AND, OR, NOT\n *                 • Grouping:           ( ... )\n *                 • Phrase search:      \"open source\"\n *                 • Field scoping:      title:security  body:\"zero trust\"\n *\n *               The module is responsible for\n *                 1. Tokenising raw, user-supplied query strings\n *                 2. Building an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST)\n *                 3. Translating the AST into a SQL fragment that can be\n *                    embedded safely (with positional parameters) in queries\n *                    issued by our ORM layer.\n *\n *               All public APIs are contained under namespace:\n *                   IntraLedger::Search\n *\n *               The code follows modern C++20 best-practices, makes heavy use\n *               of RAII, std::variant, and strong type safety, while paying\n *               particular attention to predictable performance and security\n *               (e.g. SQL-injection prevention through bound parameters).\n *\n *  REFACTORING NOTE:\n *               Core search indexing and query functionality for in-memory\n *               operations has been consolidated into the centralized\n *               SearchService (src/search/SearchService.h). This module\n *               continues to provide SQL-translation capabilities for\n *               database-based search queries.\n *\n *  ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Copyright © 2024 IntraLedger\n *  SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1\n */\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <charconv>\n#include <iomanip>\n#include <iostream>\n#include <memory>\n#include <optional>\n#include <sstream>\n#include <stdexcept>\n#include <string>\n#include <string_view>\n#include <utility>\n#include <variant>\n#include <vector>\n\n// Include the centralized SearchService for in-memory index operations\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\nnamespace IntraLedger::Search\n{\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Exceptions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass ParseError : public std::runtime_error\n{\npublic:\n    ParseError(std::string_view message, std::size_t position)\n        : std::runtime_error(fmt(message, position)), m_position(position)\n    {}\n\n    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t position() const noexcept { return m_position; }\n\nprivate:\n    static std::string fmt(std::string_view msg, std::size_t pos)\n    {\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        oss << \"ParseError @ \" << pos << \": \" << msg;\n        return oss.str();\n    }\n\n    std::size_t m_position;\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Lexer\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nenum class TokenType\n{\n    Word,\n    Phrase,\n    And,\n    Or,\n    Not,\n    Colon,\n    LParen,\n    RParen,\n    EndOfInput\n};\n\nstruct Token\n{\n    TokenType       type;\n    std::string     lexeme;\n    std::size_t     position;   // byte offset in original query string\n};\n\nclass Lexer\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Lexer(std::string_view input) : m_input(input), m_cursor(0) {}\n\n    [[nodiscard]] const Token& peek() const { return m_current; }\n\n    [[nodiscard]] Token next()\n    {\n        Token t = m_current;\n        m_current = lex();\n        return t;\n    }\n\n    void consume() { m_current = lex(); }\n\n    void reset()\n    {\n        m_cursor  = 0;\n        m_current = lex();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::string_view m_input;\n    std::size_t      m_cursor;\n    Token            m_current { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", 0 };\n\n    // Skip whitespace but keep track of cursor\n    void skip_ws()\n    {\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n    }\n\n    // Read characters while predicate holds\n    template <typename Pred>\n    std::string read_while(Pred p)\n    {\n        const auto start = m_cursor;\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && p(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n        return std::string { m_input.substr(start, m_cursor - start) };\n    }\n\n    Token lex()\n    {\n        skip_ws();\n        if (m_cursor >= m_input.size())\n            return { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", m_cursor };\n\n        const char ch = m_input[m_cursor];\n\n        // Punctuation tokens\n        switch (ch)\n        {\n            case ':':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::Colon, \":\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '(':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::LParen, \"(\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case ')':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::RParen, \")\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '\"':\n                return lex_phrase();\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        // Word or reserved keyword\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch)) || ch == '_' || ch == '-')\n        {\n            auto word = read_while([](unsigned char c) {\n                return std::isalnum(c) || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '.';\n            });\n\n            const auto lower = to_lower(word);\n            if (lower == \"and\")\n                return { TokenType::And, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"or\")\n                return { TokenType::Or, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"not\")\n                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n\n            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n    }\n\n    Token lex_phrase()\n    {\n        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        bool escaped = false;\n\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n        {\n            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n            if (escaped)\n            {\n                // Accept common escape sequences or keep char as is\n                switch (ch)\n                {\n                    case '\"':  oss << '\"'; break;\n                    case '\\\\': oss << '\\\\'; break;\n                    case 'n':  oss << '\\n'; break;\n                    case 't':  oss << '\\t'; break;\n                    default:   oss << ch;  break;\n                }\n                escaped = false;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\\\\')\n            {\n                escaped = true;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\"')\n            {\n                // End of phrase\n                return { TokenType::Phrase, oss.str(), start_pos };\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                oss << ch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Reached end-of-input without closing quote\n        throw ParseError { \"Unterminated string literal\", start_pos };\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  AST definitions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct QueryNode;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n\nstruct Term\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct Phrase\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct FieldFilter\n{\n    std::string field;  // e.g. \"title\"\n    NodePtr     arg;    // Term or Phrase\n};\n\nstruct Not\n{\n    NodePtr arg;\n};\n\nstruct And\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct Or\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct QueryNode : std::variant<Term, Phrase, FieldFilter, Not, And, Or>\n{\n    using variant::variant;\n\n    // Helper predicates\n    bool is_logical() const\n    {\n        return std::holds_alternative<And>(*this) || std::holds_alternative<Or>(*this)\n               || std::holds_alternative<Not>(*this);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Parser (recursive-descent)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass Parser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Parser(std::string_view input) : m_lexer(input)\n    {\n        m_lexer.reset(); // initialise first token\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse()\n    {\n        auto expr = parse_or();\n        expect(TokenType::EndOfInput);\n        return expr;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    Lexer m_lexer;\n\n    // Highest level: OR\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_and();\n        while (match(TokenType::Or))\n        {\n            auto rhs = parse_and();\n            node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Or { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n        }\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Intermediate level: AND (implicit or explicit)\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_unary();\n\n        while (true)\n        {\n            if (match(TokenType::And))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            // Implicit AND: two consecutive terms / groups\n            else if (peek_any({ TokenType::Word, TokenType::Phrase, TokenType::LParen, TokenType::Not }))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Unary: NOT\n    NodePtr parse_unary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::Not))\n        {\n            auto arg = parse_unary();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Not { std::move(arg) });\n        }\n        return parse_primary();\n    }\n\n    // Primary: term, phrase, parenthesis, field filter\n    NodePtr parse_primary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::LParen))\n        {\n            auto inner = parse_or();\n            expect(TokenType::RParen);\n            return inner;\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n        {\n            auto wordTok = m_lexer.next();\n\n            // Field filter?\n            if (match(TokenType::Colon))\n            {\n                const std::string fieldName = to_lower(wordTok.lexeme);\n\n                // Support only whitelisted fields\n                if (!is_whitelisted_field(fieldName))\n                    throw ParseError { \"Unknown filter field '\" + fieldName + \"'\", wordTok.position };\n\n                // Value can be phrase or term\n                NodePtr valueNode;\n                if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n                {\n                    auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode      = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n                {\n                    auto termTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode    = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { termTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    throw ParseError { \"Missing value after field filter ':'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n                }\n\n                return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(FieldFilter { fieldName, std::move(valueNode) });\n            }\n\n            /* Plain term */\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { wordTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n        {\n            auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { \"Unexpected token '\" + m_lexer.peek().lexeme + \"'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    // Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    bool match(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (peek(t))\n        {\n            m_lexer.consume();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    void expect(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (!match(t))\n            throw ParseError { \"Expected different token\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    bool peek(TokenType t) const { return m_lexer.peek().type == t; }\n\n    bool peek_any(std::initializer_list<TokenType> list) const\n    {\n        const TokenType cur = m_lexer.peek().type;\n        return std::find(list.begin(), list.end(), cur) != list.end();\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    static bool is_whitelisted_field(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        static constexpr std::array allowed = { \"title\", \"body\", \"tag\", \"author\" };\n        return std::find(allowed.begin(), allowed.end(), field) != allowed.end();\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  SQL Translation\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct SqlFragment\n{\n    std::string              sql;     // WHERE clause without the \"WHERE\" keyword\n    std::vector<std::string> params;  // positional parameters to bind in order\n};\n\nclass SqlTranslator\n{\npublic:\n    // Compile AST into SQL WHERE fragment\n    SqlFragment compile(const QueryNode& root)\n    {\n        m_buffer.clear();\n        m_params.clear();\n        walk(root);\n        return { m_buffer.str(), m_params };\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::ostringstream        m_buffer;\n    std::vector<std::string>  m_params;\n\n    // Depth-first traversal\n    void walk(const QueryNode& node)\n    {\n        std::visit([this](auto&& n) { generate(n); }, node);\n    }\n\n    // Generates SQL with PostgreSQL/MariaDB full-text search in mind.\n    // The ORM will later splice this fragment into a larger statement.\n    void generate(const Term& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Phrase& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery_phrase(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const FieldFilter& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        const std::string columnName = map_field_to_column(n.field);\n        m_buffer << columnName << \" @@ \";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Not& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"NOT (\";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const And& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" AND \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Or& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" OR \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    // Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery(const std::string& term)\n    {\n        // Simplistic sanitisation; production code would do stemming, etc.\n        std::string sanitized;\n        sanitized.reserve(term.size());\n        for (char c : term)\n        {\n            if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) || c == '_')\n                sanitized.push_back(static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)));\n        }\n        return sanitized + \":*\"; // prefix search\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery_phrase(const std::string& phrase)\n    {\n        // Replace spaces with & to enforce term adjacency in tsquery\n        std::string ts = phrase;\n        std::replace(ts.begin(), ts.end(), ' ', '&');\n        return ts;\n    }\n\n    static std::string map_field_to_column(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        if (field == \"title\")  return \"search_title\";\n        if (field == \"body\")   return \"search_body\";\n        if (field == \"tag\")    return \"search_tags\";\n        if (field == \"author\") return \"search_author\";\n        // Fallback should never happen thanks to whitelist in parser\n        throw std::logic_error(\"Unexpected field mapping request: \" + field);\n    }\n\n    void emit_param(const std::string& value)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"to_tsquery(?)\";\n        m_params.emplace_back(value);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Public facade\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass SearchCompiler\n{\npublic:\n    SqlFragment operator()(std::string_view query)\n    {\n        Parser        parser { query };\n        SqlTranslator translator;\n\n        auto ast = parser.parse();\n        return translator.compile(*ast);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Convenience functions using the centralized SearchService\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * @brief Index a document using the centralized SearchService\n *\n * This function provides a convenient way to index documents from this\n * module using the centralized SearchService.\n *\n * @param documentId The unique ID of the document to index\n * @param documentContent The text content of the document\n */\nvoid indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Query documents using the centralized SearchService\n *\n * This function provides a convenient way to search indexed documents\n * using the centralized SearchService's in-memory index.\n *\n * @param searchTerm The search query string\n * @return Vector of matching document IDs sorted by relevance\n */\nstd::vector<int> queryIndex(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Self-test (only compiled in debug / unit builds)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#ifndef NDEBUG\nstatic void self_test()\n{\n    SearchCompiler compiler;\n    std::vector<std::string> cases = {\n        R\"(security AND (title:\"zero trust\" OR body:firewall))\",\n        R\"(tag:cpp AND NOT author:\"Bad Actor\")\",\n        R\"(backup or disaster AND recovery)\",\n        R\"(\"open source\" AND (license:mit or license:gpl))\" // this will throw (invalid field)\n    };\n\n    for (const auto& q : cases)\n    {\n        try\n        {\n            auto result = compiler(q);\n            std::cout << \"\\nQuery: \" << q << \"\\nSQL:   \" << result.sql << \"\\nParams:\";\n            for (const auto& p : result.params) std::cout << \" [\" << p << \"]\";\n            std::cout << \"\\n\";\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            std::cerr << \"Error compiling query '\" << q << \"': \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Test SearchService integration\n    std::cout << \"\\nTesting SearchService integration:\\n\";\n    indexDocument(1, \"This is about security and zero trust File:        src/module_61.cpp\n *  Author:      IntraLedger Core Team\n *  Description: Implements a light-weight domain-specific language (DSL) that\n *               allows end-users to perform advanced full-text search queries\n *               similar to what you would expect from modern SaaS products.\n *\n *               Example query language features:\n *                 • Logical operators:  AND, OR, NOT\n *                 • Grouping:           ( ... )\n *                 • Phrase search:      \"open source\"\n *                 • Field scoping:      title:security  body:\"zero trust\"\n *\n *               The module is responsible for\n *                 1. Tokenising raw, user-supplied query strings\n *                 2. Building an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST)\n *                 3. Translating the AST into a SQL fragment that can be\n *                    embedded safely (with positional parameters) in queries\n *                    issued by our ORM layer.\n *\n *               All public APIs are contained under namespace:\n *                   IntraLedger::Search\n *\n *               The code follows modern C++20 best-practices, makes heavy use\n *               of RAII, std::variant, and strong type safety, while paying\n *               particular attention to predictable performance and security\n *               (e.g. SQL-injection prevention through bound parameters).\n *\n *  ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Copyright © 2024 IntraLedger\n *  SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1\n */\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <charconv>\n#include <iomanip>\n#include <iostream>\n#include <memory>\n#include <optional>\n#include <sstream>\n#include <stdexcept>\n#include <string>\n#include <string_view>\n#include <utility>\n#include <variant>\n#include <vector>\n\nnamespace IntraLedger::Search\n{\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Exceptions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass ParseError : public std::runtime_error\n{\npublic:\n    ParseError(std::string_view message, std::size_t position)\n        : std::runtime_error(fmt(message, position)), m_position(position)\n    {}\n\n    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t position() const noexcept { return m_position; }\n\nprivate:\n    static std::string fmt(std::string_view msg, std::size_t pos)\n    {\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        oss << \"ParseError @ \" << pos << \": \" << msg;\n        return oss.str();\n    }\n\n    std::size_t m_position;\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Lexer\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nenum class TokenType\n{\n    Word,\n    Phrase,\n    And,\n    Or,\n    Not,\n    Colon,\n    LParen,\n    RParen,\n    EndOfInput\n};\n\nstruct Token\n{\n    TokenType       type;\n    std::string     lexeme;\n    std::size_t     position;   // byte offset in original query string\n};\n\nclass Lexer\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Lexer(std::string_view input) : m_input(input), m_cursor(0) {}\n\n    [[nodiscard]] const Token& peek() const { return m_current; }\n\n    [[nodiscard]] Token next()\n    {\n        Token t = m_current;\n        m_current = lex();\n        return t;\n    }\n\n    void consume() { m_current = lex(); }\n\n    void reset()\n    {\n        m_cursor  = 0;\n        m_current = lex();\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::string_view m_input;\n    std::size_t      m_cursor;\n    Token            m_current { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", 0 };\n\n    // Skip whitespace but keep track of cursor\n    void skip_ws()\n    {\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n    }\n\n    // Read characters while predicate holds\n    template <typename Pred>\n    std::string read_while(Pred p)\n    {\n        const auto start = m_cursor;\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size() && p(static_cast<unsigned char>(m_input[m_cursor])))\n            ++m_cursor;\n        return std::string { m_input.substr(start, m_cursor - start) };\n    }\n\n    Token lex()\n    {\n        skip_ws();\n        if (m_cursor >= m_input.size())\n            return { TokenType::EndOfInput, \"\", m_cursor };\n\n        const char ch = m_input[m_cursor];\n\n        // Punctuation tokens\n        switch (ch)\n        {\n            case ':':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::Colon, \":\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '(':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::LParen, \"(\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case ')':\n                ++m_cursor;\n                return { TokenType::RParen, \")\", m_cursor - 1 };\n            case '\"':\n                return lex_phrase();\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        // Word or reserved keyword\n        if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(ch)) || ch == '_' || ch == '-')\n        {\n            auto word = read_while([](unsigned char c) {\n                return std::isalnum(c) || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '.';\n            });\n\n            const auto lower = to_lower(word);\n            if (lower == \"and\")\n                return { TokenType::And, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"or\")\n                return { TokenType::Or, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n            if (lower == \"not\")\n                return { TokenType::Not, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n\n            return { TokenType::Word, word, m_cursor - word.size() };\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { std::string { \"Invalid character '\" } + ch + \"'\", m_cursor };\n    }\n\n    Token lex_phrase()\n    {\n        const std::size_t start_pos = m_cursor;\n        ++m_cursor; // skip opening quote\n\n        std::ostringstream oss;\n        bool escaped = false;\n\n        while (m_cursor < m_input.size())\n        {\n            char ch = m_input[m_cursor++];\n            if (escaped)\n            {\n                // Accept common escape sequences or keep char as is\n                switch (ch)\n                {\n                    case '\"':  oss << '\"'; break;\n                    case '\\\\': oss << '\\\\'; break;\n                    case 'n':  oss << '\\n'; break;\n                    case 't':  oss << '\\t'; break;\n                    default:   oss << ch;  break;\n                }\n                escaped = false;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\\\\')\n            {\n                escaped = true;\n            }\n            else if (ch == '\"')\n            {\n                // End of phrase\n                return { TokenType::Phrase, oss.str(), start_pos };\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                oss << ch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Reached end-of-input without closing quote\n        throw ParseError { \"Unterminated string literal\", start_pos };\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  AST definitions\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct QueryNode;\n\nusing NodePtr = std::unique_ptr<QueryNode>;\n\nstruct Term\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct Phrase\n{\n    std::string value;\n};\n\nstruct FieldFilter\n{\n    std::string field;  // e.g. \"title\"\n    NodePtr     arg;    // Term or Phrase\n};\n\nstruct Not\n{\n    NodePtr arg;\n};\n\nstruct And\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct Or\n{\n    NodePtr lhs;\n    NodePtr rhs;\n};\n\nstruct QueryNode : std::variant<Term, Phrase, FieldFilter, Not, And, Or>\n{\n    using variant::variant;\n\n    // Helper predicates\n    bool is_logical() const\n    {\n        return std::holds_alternative<And>(*this) || std::holds_alternative<Or>(*this)\n               || std::holds_alternative<Not>(*this);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Parser (recursive-descent)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass Parser\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Parser(std::string_view input) : m_lexer(input)\n    {\n        m_lexer.reset(); // initialise first token\n    }\n\n    NodePtr parse()\n    {\n        auto expr = parse_or();\n        expect(TokenType::EndOfInput);\n        return expr;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    Lexer m_lexer;\n\n    // Highest level: OR\n    NodePtr parse_or()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_and();\n        while (match(TokenType::Or))\n        {\n            auto rhs = parse_and();\n            node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Or { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n        }\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Intermediate level: AND (implicit or explicit)\n    NodePtr parse_and()\n    {\n        auto node = parse_unary();\n\n        while (true)\n        {\n            if (match(TokenType::And))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            // Implicit AND: two consecutive terms / groups\n            else if (peek_any({ TokenType::Word, TokenType::Phrase, TokenType::LParen, TokenType::Not }))\n            {\n                auto rhs = parse_unary();\n                node     = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(And { std::move(node), std::move(rhs) });\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return node;\n    }\n\n    // Unary: NOT\n    NodePtr parse_unary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::Not))\n        {\n            auto arg = parse_unary();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Not { std::move(arg) });\n        }\n        return parse_primary();\n    }\n\n    // Primary: term, phrase, parenthesis, field filter\n    NodePtr parse_primary()\n    {\n        if (match(TokenType::LParen))\n        {\n            auto inner = parse_or();\n            expect(TokenType::RParen);\n            return inner;\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n        {\n            auto wordTok = m_lexer.next();\n\n            // Field filter?\n            if (match(TokenType::Colon))\n            {\n                const std::string fieldName = to_lower(wordTok.lexeme);\n\n                // Support only whitelisted fields\n                if (!is_whitelisted_field(fieldName))\n                    throw ParseError { \"Unknown filter field '\" + fieldName + \"'\", wordTok.position };\n\n                // Value can be phrase or term\n                NodePtr valueNode;\n                if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n                {\n                    auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode      = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else if (peek(TokenType::Word))\n                {\n                    auto termTok = m_lexer.next();\n                    valueNode    = std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { termTok.lexeme });\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    throw ParseError { \"Missing value after field filter ':'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n                }\n\n                return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(FieldFilter { fieldName, std::move(valueNode) });\n            }\n\n            /* Plain term */\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Term { wordTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        if (peek(TokenType::Phrase))\n        {\n            auto phraseTok = m_lexer.next();\n            return std::make_unique<QueryNode>(Phrase { phraseTok.lexeme });\n        }\n\n        throw ParseError { \"Unexpected token '\" + m_lexer.peek().lexeme + \"'\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    // Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    bool match(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (peek(t))\n        {\n            m_lexer.consume();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    void expect(TokenType t)\n    {\n        if (!match(t))\n            throw ParseError { \"Expected different token\", m_lexer.peek().position };\n    }\n\n    bool peek(TokenType t) const { return m_lexer.peek().type == t; }\n\n    bool peek_any(std::initializer_list<TokenType> list) const\n    {\n        const TokenType cur = m_lexer.peek().type;\n        return std::find(list.begin(), list.end(), cur) != list.end();\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_lower(std::string_view sv)\n    {\n        std::string out;\n        out.reserve(sv.size());\n        std::transform(sv.begin(), sv.end(), std::back_inserter(out),\n                       [](unsigned char c) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)); });\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    static bool is_whitelisted_field(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        static constexpr std::array allowed = { \"title\", \"body\", \"tag\", \"author\" };\n        return std::find(allowed.begin(), allowed.end(), field) != allowed.end();\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  SQL Translation\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct SqlFragment\n{\n    std::string              sql;     // WHERE clause without the \"WHERE\" keyword\n    std::vector<std::string> params;  // positional parameters to bind in order\n};\n\nclass SqlTranslator\n{\npublic:\n    // Compile AST into SQL WHERE fragment\n    SqlFragment compile(const QueryNode& root)\n    {\n        m_buffer.clear();\n        m_params.clear();\n        walk(root);\n        return { m_buffer.str(), m_params };\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::ostringstream        m_buffer;\n    std::vector<std::string>  m_params;\n\n    // Depth-first traversal\n    void walk(const QueryNode& node)\n    {\n        std::visit([this](auto&& n) { generate(n); }, node);\n    }\n\n    // Generates SQL with PostgreSQL/MariaDB full-text search in mind.\n    // The ORM will later splice this fragment into a larger statement.\n    void generate(const Term& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Phrase& n)\n    {\n        emit_param(to_tsquery_phrase(n.value));\n    }\n\n    void generate(const FieldFilter& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        const std::string columnName = map_field_to_column(n.field);\n        m_buffer << columnName << \" @@ \";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Not& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"NOT (\";\n        walk(*n.arg);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const And& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" AND \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    void generate(const Or& n)\n    {\n        m_buffer << '(';\n        walk(*n.lhs);\n        m_buffer << \" OR \";\n        walk(*n.rhs);\n        m_buffer << ')';\n    }\n\n    // Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery(const std::string& term)\n    {\n        // Simplistic sanitisation; production code would do stemming, etc.\n        std::string sanitized;\n        sanitized.reserve(term.size());\n        for (char c : term)\n        {\n            if (std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) || c == '_')\n                sanitized.push_back(static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c)));\n        }\n        return sanitized + \":*\"; // prefix search\n    }\n\n    static std::string to_tsquery_phrase(const std::string& phrase)\n    {\n        // Replace spaces with & to enforce term adjacency in tsquery\n        std::string ts = phrase;\n        std::replace(ts.begin(), ts.end(), ' ', '&');\n        return ts;\n    }\n\n    static std::string map_field_to_column(const std::string& field)\n    {\n        if (field == \"title\")  return \"search_title\";\n        if (field == \"body\")   return \"search_body\";\n        if (field == \"tag\")    return \"search_tags\";\n        if (field == \"author\") return \"search_author\";\n        // Fallback should never happen thanks to whitelist in parser\n        throw std::logic_error(\"Unexpected field mapping request: \" + field);\n    }\n\n    void emit_param(const std::string& value)\n    {\n        m_buffer << \"to_tsquery(?)\";\n        m_params.emplace_back(value);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Public facade\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass SearchCompiler\n{\npublic:\n    SqlFragment operator()(std::string_view query)\n    {\n        Parser        parser { query };\n        SqlTranslator translator;\n\n        auto ast = parser.parse();\n        return translator.compile(*ast);\n    }\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Self-test (only compiled in debug / unit builds)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#ifndef NDEBUG\nstatic void self_test()\n{\n    SearchCompiler compiler;\n    std::vector<std::string> cases = {\n        R\"(security AND (title:\"zero trust\" OR body:firewall))\",\n        R\"(tag:cpp AND NOT author:\"Bad Actor\")\",\n        R\"(backup or disaster AND recovery)\",\n        R\"(\"open source\" AND (license:mit or license:gpl))\" // this will throw (invalid field)\n    };\n\n    for (const auto& q : cases)\n    {\n        try\n        {\n            auto result = compiler(q);\n            std::cout << \"\\nQuery: \" << q << \"\\nSQL:   \" << result.sql << \"\\nParams:\";\n            for (const auto& p : result.params) std::cout << \" [\" << p << \"]\";\n            std::cout << \"\\n\";\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            std::cerr << \"Error compiling query '\" << q << \"': \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct SelfTestRegistrator\n{\n    SelfTestRegistrator() { self_test(); }\n} _registrator;\n\n#endif // NDEBUG\n\n} // namespace IntraLedger::Search\n```"}}

It provides methods for indexing documents and executing search\n * queries against the indexed content.\n *\n * Thread-safety: All public methods are thread-safe for concurrent access.\n */\nclass SearchService {\npublic:\n    static SearchService& instance();\n    void indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent);\n    std::vector<int> query(const std::string& searchTerm);\n    void removeDocument(int documentId);\n    void clearIndex();\n    std::size_t documentCount() const;\n\nprivate:\n    SearchService() = default;\n    ~SearchService() = default;\n    static std::vector<std::string> tokenize(const std::string& text);\n\n    std::map<std::string, std::vector<int>> invertedIndex;\n    std::unordered_set<int> indexedDocuments;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex indexMutex;\n};\n```\n\n### SearchService Implementation (SearchService.cpp)\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/search/SearchService.cpp\n// Lines: 26-86\n\nvoid SearchService::indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent) {\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(documentContent);\n    std::unordered_set<std::string> uniqueTokens(tokens.begin(), tokens.end());\n\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n    // If document was previously indexed, remove old entries\n    if (indexedDocuments.count(documentId) > 0) {\n        for (auto& [token, docIds] : invertedIndex) {\n            auto it = std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId);\n            if (it != docIds.end()) {\n                docIds.erase(it);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add new index entries\n    for (const auto& token : uniqueTokens) {\n        auto& docIds = invertedIndex[token];\n        if (std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId) == docIds.end()) {\n            docIds.push_back(documentId);\n        }\n    }\n\n    indexedDocuments.insert(documentId);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> SearchService::query(const std::string& searchTerm) {\n    if (searchTerm.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(searchTerm);\n    if (tokens.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::unordered_map<int, std::size_t> scoreTable;\n    {\n        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n        for (const auto& token : tokens) {\n            auto it = invertedIndex.find(token);\n            if (it != invertedIndex.end()) {\n                for (int docId : it->second) {\n                    ++scoreTable[docId];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::size_t>> ranked(scoreTable.begin(), scoreTable.end());\n    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n              [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second > b.second; });\n\n    std::vector<int> result;\n    result.reserve(ranked.size());\n    for (const auto& [docId, score] : ranked) {\n        result.push_back(docId);\n    }\n    return result;\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_18.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_18.cpp\n// Lines: 38, 562-579\n\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\n// ...\n\n/**\n * @brief Index a blog post document using the centralized SearchService\n */\nvoid indexBlogPost(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Query indexed documents using the centralized SearchService\n */\nstd::vector<int> searchBlogPosts(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return `src/search/` is a thread-safe singleton that manages an in-memory inverted index. It provides two primary methods:\n   - `indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)` - Tokenizes content and adds it to the inverted index\n   - `query(const std::string& searchTerm)` - Searches the index and returns matching document IDs sorted by relevance\n\n2. **module_18.cpp (Query Parser)**: This module continues to provide boolean-aware query parsing for SQL generation, but now also exposes convenience functions (`indexBlogPost`, `searchBlogPosts`) that delegate to the centralized SearchService.\n\n3. **module_41.cpp (Search Subsystem)**: This module retains SQL-based search capabilities through `DatabaseSearchService` but now also integrates with the centralized SearchService for fast in-memory index operations via `searchIndex()` and `indexDocument()` methods.\n\n4. **module_61.cpp (Search DSL)**: This module retains SQL translation capabilities for complex field-scoped queries but now also provides `indexDocument()` and `queryIndex()` functions that use the centralized SearchService.\n\n### Architectural Pattern Used\n\n- **Singleton Pattern**: The `SearchService` uses the singleton pattern via `instance()` to ensure a single shared index across all modules\n- **Thread-Safe Design**: Uses `std::shared_mutex` for read-write locking to allow concurrent reads while ensuring exclusive writes\n- **Inverted Index**: Implements a classic inverted index data structure (`std::map<std::string, std::vector<int>>`) for efficient full-text search\n- **TF Scoring**: Documents are scored by term frequency (number of matching search terms) and returned in descending order of relevance\n\n### Dependencies and Relationships\n\n```\n                     ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                     │   SearchService         │\n                     │   (src/search/)         │\n                     │                         │\n                     │ - SearchService::indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent) {\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(documentContent);\n    std::unordered_set<std::string> uniqueTokens(tokens.begin(), tokens.end());\n\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n    // If document was previously indexed, remove old entries\n    if (indexedDocuments.count(documentId) > 0) {\n        for (auto& [token, docIds] : invertedIndex) {\n            auto it = std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId);\n            if (it != docIds.end()) {\n                docIds.erase(it);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add new index entries\n    for (const auto& token : uniqueTokens) {\n        auto& docIds = invertedIndex[token];\n        if (std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId) == docIds.end()) {\n            docIds.push_back(documentId);\n        }\n    }\n\n    indexedDocuments.insert(documentId);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> SearchService::query(const std::string& searchTerm) {\n    if (searchTerm.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(searchTerm);\n    if (tokens.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::unordered_map<int, std::size_t> scoreTable;\n    {\n        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n        for (const auto& token : tokens) {\n            auto it = invertedIndex.find(token);\n            if (it != invertedIndex.end()) {\n                for (int docId : it->second) {\n                    ++scoreTable[docId];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::size_t>> ranked(scoreTable.begin(), scoreTable.end());\n    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n              [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second > b.second; });\n\n    std::vector<int> result;\n    result.reserve(ranked.size());\n    for (const auto& [docId, score] : ranked) {\n        result.push_back(docId);\n    }\n    return result;\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_18.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_18.cpp\n// Lines: 38, 562-579\n\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\n// ...\n\n/**\n * @brief Index a blog post document using the centralized SearchService\n */\nvoid indexBlogPost(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Query indexed documents using the centralized SearchService\n */\nstd::vector<int> searchBlogPosts(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_41.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_41.cpp\n// Lines: 63-64, 506-517, 557-568\n\n// Include the centralized SearchService\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\n// In DatabaseSearchService class:\nstd::vector<int> searchIndex(const std::string& query)\n{\n    return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(query);\n}\n\nvoid indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& content)\n{\n    intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, content);\n}\n\n// Convenience functions:\nvoid indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> queryDocuments(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_61.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_61.cpp\n// Lines: 59-60, 628-645\n\n// Include the centralized SearchService for in-memory index operations\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\n// ...\n\nvoid indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> queryIndex(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return intraledger::search::SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### How the Components Interact\n\n1. **SearchService (Centralized Core)**: The new `SearchService` class in `src/search/` is a thread-safe singleton that manages an in-memory inverted index. It provides two primary methods:\n   - `indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)` - Tokenizes content and adds it to the inverted index\n   - `query(const std::string& searchTerm)` - Searches the index and returns matching document IDs sorted by relevance\n\n2. **module_18.cpp (Query Parser)**: This module continues to provide boolean-aware query parsing for SQL generation, but now also exposes convenience functions (`indexBlogPost`, `searchBlogPosts`) that delegate to the centralized SearchService.\n\n3. **module_41.cpp (Search Subsystem)**: This module retains SQL-based search capabilities through `DatabaseSearchService` but now also integrates with the centralized SearchService for fast in-memory index operations via `searchIndex()` and `indexDocument()` methods.\n\n4. **module_61.cpp (Search DSL)**: This module retains SQL translation capabilities for complex field-scoped queries but now also provides `indexDocument()` and `queryIndex()` functions that use the centralized SearchService.\n\n### Architectural Pattern Used\n\n- **Singleton Pattern**: The `SearchService` uses the singleton pattern via `instance()` to ensure a single shared index across all modules\n- **Thread-Safe Design**: Uses `std::shared_mutex` for read-write locking to allow concurrent reads while ensuring exclusive writes\n- **Inverted Index**: Implements a classic inverted index data structure (`std::map<std::string, std::vector<int>>`) for efficient full-text search\n- **TF Scoring**: Documents are scored by term frequency (number of matching search terms) and returned in descending order of relevance\n\n### Dependencies and Relationships\n\n```\n                     ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                     │   SearchService         │\n                     │   (src/search/)         │\n                     │                         │\n                     │ - invertedIndex         │\n                     │ - indexDocument()       │\n                     │ - query()               │\n                     └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                 │\n        ┌────────────────────────┼────────────────────────┐\n        │                        │                        │\n        ▼                        ▼                        ▼\n┌───────────────┐      ┌───────────────┐      ┌───────────────┐\n│ module_18.cpp │      │ module_41.cpp │      │ module_61.cpp │\n│               │      │               │      │               │\n│ Query Parser  │      │ Database      │      │ DSL Parser    │\n│ SQL Generator │      │ Search Service│      │ SQL Translator│\n│               │      │               │      │               │\n│ indexBlogPost │      │ indexDocument │      │ indexDocument │\n│ searchBlogPosts│      │ searchIndex  │      │ queryIndex    │\n└───────────────┘      └───────────────┘      └───────────────┘\n```\n\n## Implementation\n\n### Files Created\n\n1. **src/search/SearchService.h** (108 lines)\n   - uniqueTokens(tokens.begin(), tokens.end());\n\n    std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n\n    // If document was previously indexed, remove old entries\n    if (indexedDocuments.count(documentId) > 0) {\n        for (auto& [token, docIds] : invertedIndex) {\n            auto it = std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId);\n            if (it != docIds.end()) {\n                docIds.erase(it);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add new index entries\n    for (const auto& token : uniqueTokens) {\n        auto& docIds = invertedIndex[token];\n        if (std::find(docIds.begin(), docIds.end(), documentId) == docIds.end()) {\n            docIds.push_back(documentId);\n        }\n    }\n\n    indexedDocuments.insert(documentId);\n}\n\nstd::vector<int> SearchService::query(const std::string& searchTerm) {\n    if (searchTerm.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::string> tokens = tokenize(searchTerm);\n    if (tokens.empty()) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::unordered_map<int, std::size_t> scoreTable;\n    {\n        std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(indexMutex);\n        for (const auto& token : tokens) {\n            auto it = invertedIndex.find(token);\n            if (it != invertedIndex.end()) {\n                for (int docId : it->second) {\n                    ++scoreTable[docId];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::size_t>> ranked(scoreTable.begin(), scoreTable.end());\n    std::sort(ranked.begin(), ranked.end(),\n              [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second > b.second; });\n\n    std::vector<int> result;\n    result.reserve(ranked.size());\n    for (const auto& [docId, score] : ranked) {\n        result.push_back(docId);\n    }\n    return result;\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_18.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_18.cpp\n// Lines: 38, 562-579\n\n#include \"search/SearchService.h\"\n\n// ...\n\n/**\n * @brief Index a blog post document using the centralized SearchService\n */\nvoid indexBlogPost(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)\n{\n    SearchService::instance().indexDocument(documentId, documentContent);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Query indexed documents using the centralized SearchService\n */\nstd::vector<int> searchBlogPosts(const std::string& searchTerm)\n{\n    return SearchService::instance().query(searchTerm);\n}\n```\n\n### Integration in module_41.cpp\n```cpp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_41.cpp\n// Lines: 63-64, 506-517, 557-568\n\n// Include the `src/search/` is a thread-safe singleton that manages an in-memory inverted index. It provides two primary methods:\n   - `indexDocument(int documentId, const std::string& documentContent)` - Tokenizes content and adds it to the inverted index\n   - `query(const std::string& searchTerm)` - Searches the index and returns matching document IDs sorted by relevance\n\n2. **module_18.cpp (Query Parser)**: This module continues to provide boolean-aware query parsing for SQL generation, but now also exposes convenience functions (`indexBlogPost`, `searchBlogPosts`) that delegate to the centralized SearchService.\n\n3. **module_41.cpp (Search Subsystem)**: This module retains SQL-based search capabilities through `DatabaseSearchService` but now also integrates with the centralized SearchService for fast in-memory index operations via `searchIndex()` and `indexDocument()` methods.\n\n4. **module_61.cpp (Search DSL)**: This module retains SQL translation capabilities for complex field-scoped queries but now also provides `indexDocument()` and `queryIndex()` functions that use the centralized SearchService.\n\n### Architectural Pattern Used\n\n- **Singleton Pattern**: The `SearchService` uses the singleton pattern via `instance()` to ensure a single shared index across all modules\n- **Thread-Safe Design**: Uses `std::shared_mutex` for read-write locking to allow concurrent reads while ensuring exclusive writes\n- **Inverted Index**: Implements a classic inverted index data structure (`std::map<std::string, std::vector<int>>`) for efficient full-text search\n- **TF Scoring**: Documents are scored by term frequency (number of matching search terms) and returned in descending order of relevance\n\n### Dependencies and Relationships\n\n```\n                     ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                     │   SearchService         │\n                     │   (src/search/)         │\n                     │                         │\n                     │ -
